Planning is BLOCKS-FIRST, not prose-first. Each wipnote:plan interview stage authors a visual block inline as the slice YAML is built: Scope -> file-tree, API/contract -> api-endpoint + data-model, UI/flow -> wireframe/diagram; what/why/done_when DERIVE from the blocks. arch:capability-delivery-tiersdecisionactive
plugin/**
cmd/wipnote/prompts/**
feat-9e03e3f7
claude-code/feat-9e03e3f7
When delivering a wipnote capability, pick the cheapest context tier that works: CLI-via-Bash (near-zero resident cost) > Skill (progressive disclosure, body loads on invoke) > deferred MCP tool (names only until used) > eager MCP tool (full schema always resident; avoid). Never expose wipnote own command surface as eager MCP tools — that recreates MCP context bloat (a 3-server/40-tool setup burned ~72 percent of context on schemas before the first prompt). MCP is for external user-chosen services only, with deferred/tool-search loading. The CLI tier is also the most cross-harness-portable: deferred MCP loading is default on Claude Code 2.1.x but unconfirmed on Codex/Antigravity. Verified Feb 2026 (Anthropic Tool Search / defer_loading).

arch:claude-dir-sync-scopeinvariantactive
.claude/agent-memory/**
spk-2806f862
spk-2806f862
No wipnote code path copies plugin/agents, skills or commands into the repo-root .claude/ directory. The Claude target's outDir is plugin/, and build-ports only writes plugin/.claude-plugin/plugin.json and plugin/hooks/hooks.json. The only delete-on-sync logic (removeMarketplaceWipnote in cmd/wipnote/claude.go) operates on the USER HOME tree ~/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/ and cache/, never on repo-root .claude/. So project docs saying '.claude/ auto-synced from plugin/, changes are lost' refer to the marketplace copy and generated plugin files, NOT to .claude/agent-memory/, which is untouched. Note Claude Code namespaces plugin agent memory as <plugin>-<agent>, e.g. wipnote-researcher, not the bare frontmatter name. Verified 2026-08-07.
arch:codex-hook-event-vocabularyinvariantactive
packages/plugin-core/manifest.json
port/pluginbuild/**
bug-e39d408f
bug-e39d408f
Codex CLI 0.147.0 fires exactly 11 hook events: SessionStart, SessionEnd, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, PermissionRequest, PreCompact, PostCompact, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, Stop. Verified three ways — HookEventNameWire enum and 11 embedded JSON schemas extracted from the Rust binary, a live black-box hooks.json test, and learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks. TaskStarted and TaskComplete are PHANTOM: zero occurrences across 533k extracted binary strings. TurnAborted is real but is an internal ThreadEvent telemetry variant, not a dispatchable hook. Critically, Codex SILENTLY DROPS unrecognized event names at dispatch — no warning, no error, and codex doctor has no hooks diagnostic. Nothing upstream will catch a typo.
arch:codex-hook-payload-shapeinvariantactive
core/hooks/harness.go
bug-9f49a375
bug-9f49a375
Captured from real codex-cli 0.147.0 payloads on 2026-08-07. Codex PostToolUse uses the key tool_response, never tool_result (4 of 4 captures). Its VALUE is a bare JSON string of raw tool stdout, not an object as Claude Code sends — the previous map[string]any field would have thrown a decode error on every non-empty output, not silently discarded it. Fix uses custom UnmarshalJSON accepting either shape, honouring both key spellings. task_id and task_subject are NOT hook-payload fields: task_id appears only in Codex Cloud's RawRecord task-tracking schema, task_subject appears nowhere in the binary. Misreading Codex Cloud's task vocabulary as hook vocabulary is the likely origin of the phantom TaskStarted/TaskComplete registrations.

arch:codex-plugin-hooks-delivery-is-deadhazardretired
cmd/wipnote/codex.go
bug-e39d408f
bug-e39d408f
codex features list shows plugin_hooks at stage 'removed', value false, and it cannot be re-enabled. A hooks.json bundled inside a Codex plugin via the marketplace route NEVER fires, trust-bypass or not — verified empirically against the OpenAI-curated figma and replayio plugins. wipnote is unaffected because ensureCodexGlobalHooksInstalled mirrors the bundled hooks.json directly into ~/.codex/hooks.json on launch, which is the route proven to work. Do NOT 'simplify' this by letting Codex auto-load the plugin's own hooks.json; that path is inert in current Codex.
arch:codex-plugin-hooks-do-fireinvariantactive
cmd/wipnote/codex.go
spk-d62bd485
spk-d62bd485
CORRECTS the retired card codex-plugin-hooks-delivery-is-dead. Plugin-bundled hooks.json DOES fire in codex-cli 0.147.0 — proven with a scratch CODEX_HOME carrying NO mirror, toggling plugin enabled true/false/true (blocked, allowed, blocked), reproduced twice. The 'plugin_hooks: removed/false' flag is a DEAD NO-OP, not a sunset: other removed-stage flags are frozen true and unambiguously live (sqlite, steer, tui_app_server), so removed means the flag stopped being checked, kept only for config compatibility. Passing --disable plugin_hooks explicitly still blocked. learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks documents plugin hooks as first-class with a trust model and PLUGIN_ROOT env vars; changelog is silent. Treat the plugin route as durable.
arch:deferred-completes-exec-branch-provenancedecisionactive
trk-6a1f5362
claude-code/claude-opus-4-8
Multi-slice plans run on a separate exec branch and merged back (squash/rebase) get per-item commit SHAs rewritten; code commits on main aren't work-item-ID-tagged. So 'wipnote trace <id>' shows 0 commits and 'complete' refuses with 'zero linked source commits' though the work is merged. link-commit needs a SHA that no longer exists, and files overlap across slices so per-item attribution isn't clean. Resolution: complete with --accepted-advisory recording the merge-back rationale (auditable via 'wipnote check accepted-advisory'; reversible; link-commit later if SHAs reconstructed). Don't guess SHAs — wrong links are worse than an honest advisory. Confirmed on trk-6a1f5362 / plan-2390966a: 12 items closed via advisory.


arch:durable-architecture-bets-2026-06-13decisionactive
core/**
plugin/**
feat-9e03e3f7
claude-code/feat-9e03e3f7
Durable design validated by research (local-first; event-sourcing/CQRS; SQLite single-writer). KEEP: HTML+NDJSON canonical store, SQLite disposable projection, git as durability/sync, daemon single-writer. Reinforce: merge=union on NDJSON logs, idempotent reindex, fsck integrity check. AVOID (single-machine YAGNI): CRDTs, sync engines, event-store servers. Cross-harness portability = generated per-harness plugin trees plus harness-neutral session/lineage model (the moat), NOT AGENTS.md. MCP = external interop only with deferred loading. Capability delivery tiers: see capability-delivery-tiers card. Boundary: see plugin-project-boundary card. Align-later (do not block): OTel GenAI / OpenInference export. External deadline: Gemini CLI to Antigravity CLI 2026-06-18 (verify port mechanics first). Supersedes the looser bet-on-AGENTS.md-and-MCP framing.


arch:edge-href-collection-awareinvariantactive
core/workitem/htmlwriter.go
core/workitem/templates/node.gohtml
core/htmlparse/parser.go
architect-coder
Work-item HTML edge links MUST be collection-aware: edgeHref() in core/workitem/htmlwriter.go maps the target ID prefix to its .wipnote subdir (feat->features, bug->bugs, spk->spikes, trk->tracks, pln->plans, spc->specs, UUID-shaped->sessions). A bare <id>.html href only resolves within the same collection dir and 404s cross-collection. The htmlparse parser strips any leading directory when reading hrefs back, so prefixed hrefs round-trip to the same target ID.

arch:go-tmpdir-hazardhazardactive
**/*.go
go.mod
go.sum
eababf24b1e0a4f249ce63762026d0e9f372a6f2claude-code/feat-c96e069b
Before any `go build`, `go vet`, or `go test` in this repo, run: `export TMPDIR=$HOME/.gotest-tmp GOTMPDIR=$HOME/.gotest-tmp/wipnote-build && mkdir -p "$TMPDIR" "$GOTMPDIR"`. Lease tests (TestLease_AcquireAndHold, TestSingleOwnerLeaseRace, TestStaleSocketUnlinkRefusedWhenDifferentOwnerAlive) require a temp path containing "wipnote" outside a git tree; default /tmp false-fails them. Re-export in every shell call — state does not persist between calls.


arch:goja-dagre-layout-is-viabledecisionactive
plan/blocks/svg/**
plan-f8c02547
plan-f8c02547
goja executes the unmodified 283KB dagre.js bundle with only d2's 7-line setup.js plus a console shim - no window or document scaffolding. d2's lib/jsrunner/goja.go on the non-wasm build tag is itself backed by dop251/goja, so this is direct precedent. Measured: goja.Compile 10.9ms once, fresh runtime plus precompiled program 1.3-2ms, layout 16.6ms at 5 nodes and 106ms at 16 (corpus max). CGO_ENABLED=0 builds clean, 4 transitive deps. Implementation rules: precompile behind sync.Once; batch extraction via two Array.map calls rather than d2's per-node JSON.stringify round-trips; scope one render to a single plan or track, since cost is ~O(n^1.3) and crosses 1s near 100 nodes.

433ca649d7fb6a0fc0d436326bb619d5377ebdbewipnote-completion
Attribution lives in TWO stores with a precedence rule: active_work_items (root agent) wins over sessions.active_feature_id. Mirror GetActiveWorkItemWithFallback semantics everywhere.

c27dcc87005291939ceff437ed96fba0e561f9b4wipnote-completion
Installing over a running executable: write a sibling temp in the destination dir (same filesystem) + chmod + os.Rename. rename() swaps the directory entry atomically and avoids ETXTBSY because the running process keeps its old inode until exit. a1476bf6e73dabe36e3773cd291eddf32043b529wipnote-completion
Claude Code splits a logical session: SessionStart fires only on a short parent stub (which gets active_feature_id), while the long-running child session IDs (019ede…) never get a SessionStart the hook sees — so they carry no work-item attribution and are dropped by the resumable query's work_item_id <> '' gate (session_repo.go:705) before any other filter. Fix is twofold: (1) a current-session chooser slot resolved from harness/launcher env + session-family members that bypasses BOTH the work_item_id and item_status('done','completed') gates, and (2) PropagateFamilyAttribution wired into SessionStart copying the family's work item onto siblings lacking one. Lean on family membership (session_family_id / session-families.json) as the durable parent-child link — never assume the hook fires per child.

arch:plan-blocks-have-no-drawing-primitivedecisionactive

Across the whole plan corpus, 34 rendered block instances contain ZERO svg or canvas elements. This is by explicit design: plan/blocks/diagram.go states it is 'deliberately Mermaid-free... no SVG text-measurement'. Consequence per type: diagram renders bordered divs joined by a Unicode arrow glyph, not drawn lines; file-tree has no indentation logic in template or CSS, so 3 files and 30 files render identically flat; wireframe is colored text lines; data-model and api-endpoint are tables. The catalog is a text-layout vocabulary, not a visual one. Separately, the dependency graph's svg is EMPTY in the static document - d3 plus dagre-d3 build it client-side from a display:none data island, so it is invisible in archived or no-JS views. (spk-52c8a7a8)


arch:plan-bulk-is-perslice-proseinvariantactive
plan/planyaml/schema.go
spk-52c8a7a8
spk-52c8a7a8
Measured over 45 plans and 282 slices: what, done_when, why and tests account for 74.8% of all slice words; what alone is 33% at a median 96.5 words per slice. Blocks are 0.4% of words. Median plan reads in 9.3 min, worst observed 27 min. The trend that matters: between the oldest and newest halves of the corpus, slices per plan fell 22% while words per slice rose 77% - plans are not getting structurally complex, individual slices are getting verbose. Worst cluster hits 796-1239 words per slice from what plus questions plus critic_revisions stacking across critique rounds, i.e. critique appends rather than revises. (spk-52c8a7a8)


arch:plan-html-is-the-retrieval-corpushazardactive
cmd/wipnote/relevant.go
cmd/wipnote/find.go
spk-3cc71f1c
spk-3cc71f1c
wipnote relevant shells out to ripgrep with --type html, so .wipnote/plans/*.yaml is NEVER scanned; hits resolve via goquery on article[id][data-type][data-status] emitted by plan_page.gohtml. wipnote find plans loads via core/graph.LoadDir, which silently skips every non-.html file. Consequence: the generated plan HTML is a retrieval index, not just a view. Ceasing to emit it would make both commands blind to all 42 plans with no error - just zero matches. Any renderer change must repoint retrieval at YAML prose fields (what/why/decisions_notes/blocks) BEFORE or alongside, never after. Note wipnote history plan-<id> already reads .yaml, an existing inconsistency. (spk-3cc71f1c)


arch:plan-implemented-in-edge-not-reindexablehazardactive
cmd/wipnote/reindex_plan_edges.go
spk-3cc71f1c
spk-3cc71f1c
The plan->track implemented_in edge is written by PlanCollection.AddEdge into SQLite graph_edges and patched into the plan HTML nav data-graph-edges block. But reindexPlanEdges rebuilds only planned_in and blocked_by from YAML slices, and the generic reindexEdges pass deliberately excludes the plans directory. So no reindex path recovers implemented_in from either YAML or HTML. If SQLite is wiped and rebuilt, the edge silently disappears from lineage and trace despite being present in committed HTML. PlanMeta.TrackID already holds the data; teaching reindexPlanEdges to emit implemented_in from it is the likely fix. Pre-existing, independent of any renderer change. (spk-3cc71f1c)


arch:plan-is-a-nested-go-modulehazardactive
plan/go.mod
plan-f8c02547
plan-f8c02547
The repo has FIVE Go modules: root, core/, observe/, plan/, and port/. A root-level 'go build ./... && go vet ./... && go test ./...' covers NONE of the four nested ones — it reports green while any of them is broken. Gate each explicitly, e.g. (cd plan && go build ./... && go vet ./... && go test ./...), and the same for core, observe and port. Discovered during feat-b7fdd8d9: the new plan/blocks/svg package built clean under a scoped run but was invisible to the root gate, and 'go test ./plan/...' from root does not reach it either. The quality-gate snippet in project docs describes the root module only.


arch:plan-review-ui-embedded-in-htmlhazardactive
plan/plantmpl/**
cmd/wipnote/api_plans.go
spk-3cc71f1c
spk-3cc71f1c
Approve/reject radios, comment textareas, block annotations and chat are ~1100 lines of inline JS in plan_page.gohtml, wired to data-section, data-action, data-question and data-block-anchor attributes plus the anchor scheme slice-N-block-type-idx, POSTing to /api/plans/{id}/feedback. The backend is DOM-agnostic, but only that emitted HTML can construct valid section keys - no other client exists. The plan renderer is therefore not swappable until review becomes a documented API client. Two further leaks: the graph-edges nav is workflow state with no YAML representation, surviving re-render only via snapshot/restore string matching; and finalizePlanHTML plus isCRISPIPlanFile couple to literal DOM markers with no shared Go type, so markup changes break them silently.


arch:plan-rewrite-preserve-trackinvariantactive
cmd/wipnote/plan_yaml_extras.go
plan/planyaml/schema.go
architect-coder
wipnote plan rewrite-yaml MUST preserve the existing plan's meta.track_id (planyaml.PlanMeta.TrackID) when the incoming YAML omits it. The track link is structural; LLM/hand rewrites of the plan body routinely drop it, which silently severs the plan from its track and makes finalize create a NEW track instead of attaching to the existing one (root cause of plan-edeb2163 losing trk-23232c8d). preserveTrackLinkage() carries the existing track_id forward; an explicit non-empty track_id in the rewrite is respected for intentional re-targeting. The canonical meta field is 'track_id', not 'track'.

arch:plan-yaml-render-drops-html-only-statehazardactive
cmd/wipnote/plan_create.go
core/workitem/plans.go
feature-coder
commitPlanChange's renderPlanToFileQuiet/renderPlanToFile (cmd/wipnote/plan_create.go) fully regenerate a plan's HTML from the plantmpl template on every YAML-driven mutation (add-slice-yaml, set-design-yaml, rewrite-yaml, ...). plantmpl has zero concept of graph edges, so any state that lives ONLY in the HTML (not the YAML) gets silently dropped on rebuild unless explicitly snapshotted/restored. Fixed for graph edges in bug-b12b537b via renderPlanCore snapshotting existing edges (htmlparse.ParseFile) before os.Create truncates the file, then restoring via the newly-exported workitem.PatchGraphEdgesNav. If another HTML-only feature is ever added to plan nodes, it needs the same snapshot/restore treatment at the renderPlanCore choke point or it will silently vanish on the next YAML mutation.


arch:plugin-project-boundaryinvariantactive
plugin/**
packages/plugin-core/**
feat-9e03e3f7
claude-code/feat-9e03e3f7
wipnote is a plugin installed across many projects; it must never author, generate, or overwrite a project own instruction files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md). Those are user-owned, repo-committed, and describe the project — they configure nothing for the plugin. wipnote agents READ and respect whatever project-instruction file the host harness exposes, and at most OFFER an opt-in, user-reviewed snippet (init-style); never silent ownership. Cross-harness portability of wipnote BEHAVIOR comes from the single-source manifest to generated per-harness trees, NOT from AGENTS.md. Portability of PROJECT intent is the AGENTS.md ecosystem standard job. Corollary already in CLAUDE.md: never land fixes in AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md.


arch:serve-child-lifecyclesubsystem-mapactive
cmd/wipnote/serve_child.go
internal/childproc/**
e56c49a13549da970df6ea78c4dd4453b1ece6e9feat-359312ab
Child server lifecycle: started on-demand by Supervisor when the first request arrives for a project. Owns OTEL receiver, indexer, retention, daemon apply loop, and writequeue sink. Shuts down cleanly on SIGTERM via context cancellation. The _serve-child hidden cobra subcommand is the child entry point — never invoke directly; the parent spawns it via childproc.Supervisor.


arch:serve-hub-architecturesubsystem-mapactive
cmd/wipnote/serve*.go
cmd/wipnote/dashboard/**
cmd/wipnote/dashboard.go
e56c49a13549da970df6ea78c4dd4453b1ece6e9feat-359312ab
wipnote serve runs a parent+child process model: the parent (serve_parent.go) is a reverse proxy that auto-discovers registered projects and forwards /p/<id>/* traffic to per-project child processes spawned by the childproc.Supervisor. Each child (serve_child.go) owns one project's database connection, writequeue, OTEL receiver, and SSE event stream. The dashboard JS (dashboard/) connects via SSE for live updates. Parent and child communicate over local Unix/TCP sockets managed by the Supervisor.


arch:serve-parent-proxy-invariantinvariantactive
cmd/wipnote/serve_parent.go
e56c49a13549da970df6ea78c4dd4453b1ece6e9feat-359312ab
All /p/<project-id>/* routes are validated against validProjectIDRE (hex chars only) before registry lookup — defense-in-depth against path traversal. Project IDs must never contain path separators, null bytes, or non-hex characters. autoDetectCurrentProject() uses the registry to find the current project from CWD; it returns nil silently when no match exists (not an error).


arch:serve-sse-dashboardsubsystem-mapactive
cmd/wipnote/dashboard/**
cmd/wipnote/serve.go
e56c49a13549da970df6ea78c4dd4453b1ece6e9feat-359312ab
The dashboard (cmd/wipnote/dashboard/) is a static SPA served by each child's HTTP mux. It uses SSE (Server-Sent Events) for live event streaming and polls REST endpoints for status. buildSingleProjectMux() wires all routes and is shared between single-project legacy mode and the per-project child process path. Dashboard assets are embedded in the binary via Go embed.


arch:serve-writequeue-hazardhazardactive
cmd/wipnote/serve_child.go
cmd/wipnote/serve_global.go
e56c49a13549da970df6ea78c4dd4453b1ece6e9feat-359312ab
Dashboard read pool is capped (dashboardReadPoolMaxConns) to prevent connection bursts from holding too many SHARED locks that starve the single SQLite writer under DELETE journal mode (bug-74a7bda7). Never remove the pool cap or allow unbounded concurrent readers. The writer uses a writequeue.Queue to serialize writes; bypassing it causes SQLITE_BUSY at runtime.


arch:shared-tree-parallel-agent-hazardshazardactive
plugin/skills/execute/**
plan-f8c02547
plan-f8c02547
When several agents edit one working tree concurrently: (1) NEVER use git stash - one agent stashed plan/ to test a hypothesis and reverted every other agent's uncommitted work for 60-90s; it popped clean, but any agent reading files in that window saw HEAD, not its own edits. (2) Declared per-slice file lists UNDER-describe conflict surface: deleting a Go type breaks every consumer, so slice 7 removing blocks.APIEndpoint broke plan/plantmpl/blocks_zone.go and visual_overview_zone.go, neither of which appeared in any slice's files list. (3) Agents must stage explicitly by filename - never git add -A. (4) Require commit immediately on completion; uncommitted work is the exposure.

arch:writer-socket-bind-dial-divergencehazardactive
core/daemon/**
core/hooks/tooluse_shared.go
core/paths/resolve.go
bug-66d45f4e
spk-f4d48317
orchestrator
Writer daemon binds SocketPath(rawProjectRoot); hooks dial SocketPath(ResolveProjectDir(...)). Safe today (real session IDs are unique UUIDs), but the two sides resolving the project dir independently is a latent divergence: a stale session-hint (paths.SessionHintPath in shared os.TempDir) makes dial != bind, the enqueue dial misses, and hot writes fall to the direct lock-contending path. Surfaced by TestSQLiteContentionStress_MigratedHotHooksUnderHeldLock on persistent-TMPDIR hosts; fixed test-side in bug-66d45f4e (hermetic hint removal, f797e2822). Defense-in-depth: bind the daemon at the SAME resolved project dir the hooks compute (one shared resolution choke point) so bind/dial provably agree. See spk-f4d48317.
